### [48] [A Real-Time Generalized Nash Equilibrium Framework for Interaction-Aware Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21043)
*Nouhed Naidja,Mohamed-Cherif Rahal,Steve Pechberti,Stéphane Font,Guillaume Sandou,Marc Revilloud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对混合交通环境中的自动驾驶车辆决策问题，提出了一种基于广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的综合决策框架，并利用粒子群优化（PSO）求解器实现实时求解。在真实Renault Zoé与人工驾驶者的联合测试中验证了该方法，实验结果显示系统能够在50毫秒内收敛，生成舒适且类人的轨迹，有效应对复杂交通场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通环境中，自动驾驶车辆的安全高效导航面临重大挑战，主要源于车辆决策与人类驾驶员不可预测反应之间的复杂相互依赖关系。

Method: 将驾驶交互建模为广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP），显式纳入共享安全与几何约束，确保AV策略可行性动态关联对手行为；采用粒子群优化（PSO）求解该非凸问题，实现实时计算。

Result: 测试结果表明系统在50毫秒内收敛，能够生成舒适且类人的轨迹，有效处理临界场景。

Conclusion: 该框架具备实时运行可行性，能够在混合交通环境中通过舒适且类人的轨迹实现安全驾驶决策。

Abstract: Safe and efficient navigation in mixed-traffic environments remains a critical challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), primarily due to the complex interdependence between the AV's decisions and the unpredictable reactions of human drivers. This paper introduces a comprehensive decision-making framework that formulates the driving interaction as a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problem (GNEP). Unlike decoupled optimization approaches, this framework explicitly models shared safety and geometric constraints, ensuring that the feasibility of the AV's strategy is dynamically linked to the opponent's actions. To solve this non-convex problem in real-time, we propose a dedicated solver based on Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). The complete architecture was validated on a test track using a real autonomous Renault Zoé interacting with a human driver. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to handle critical scenarios by generating comfortable, human-like trajectories. Benchmarks confirm the solver's operational feasibility, achieving convergence in under 50 ms.

</details>


### [49] [RL-MACRO: A Cybernetic Closed-Loop Intelligence Framework for Multimodal Adaptive Robotic Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21113)
*Xiao Zhang,Jiaxuan Li,Renzhen Le,Di Wu,Chao Sun,Jiachen Zhu,Haoyuan Zhang,Xiang Li,Jian Liu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RL-MACRO框架，通过多模态感知与自适应决策实现自主颅骨切开术中工具-组织交互的持续调控，解决部分可观测性和温度不可测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主颅骨切开术中需持续调节工具-组织交互以减轻机械过载和热损伤并保持手术效率，但过程具有部分可观测性：组织属性未知且时变，物理遮挡下无法直接测量切割温度。

Method: RL-MACRO框架包含：1) CNN-LSTM观察器融合力觉与听觉反馈重构隐藏温度状态（R²=0.939, MAE=1.717°C）；2) 基于重构温度的多传感器特征形成信念状态，采用离线隐式Q学习(IQL)策略；3) 新型双头Actor动态协调进给速度、主轴转速和切割深度，在严格安全约束内优化效率；4) 在线轨迹重规划与速度伺服实现动作平滑执行。

Result: 在牛肋骨和6个离体山羊头骨上验证，系统表现出鲁棒感知能力（温度重构R²=0.939）、对力/温度偏差的自适应恢复能力，以及在不规则表面上的平滑执行。

Conclusion: 建立了数据驱动的安全高效自主骨切割的赛博控制范式。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic craniotomy requires continuous regulation of tool-tissue interactions to mitigate mechanical overload and thermal damage while maintaining surgical efficiency. However, this process is inherently partially observable due to unknown, time-varying tissue properties and the inability to directly measure cutting temperatures under physical occlusion. To address these challenges, we propose RL-MACRO, a cybernetic closed-loop intelligence framework that couples multimodal perception, adaptive decision-making, and robotic execution. This framework empowers the surgical robot to autonomously perceive inaccessible states from partial sensory feedback and dynamically optimize its behaviors under uncertain environment. A CNN-LSTM observer first fuses force and sound feedback to reconstruct the hidden temperature state (R^2=0.939, MAE = 1.717 deg C). This reconstructed temperature, alongside multi-sensor features, forms the belief state for an offline Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) policy. A novel dual-head Actor dynamically coordinates the feed rate, spindle speed, and cutting depth to optimize efficiency within strict safety bounds. These decisions are seamlessly translated into spatial motions via online trajectory re-planning and velocity servoing. Experiments on bovine ribs and six ex vivo goat skulls validate the system's robust perception, adaptive recovery from force/temperature excursions, and smooth execution on irregular surfaces, establishing a data-driven cybernetic paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous bone cutting.

</details>


### [50] [FORGE-plus: Force-Budgeted Recovery for Contact-Rich Assembly with a Frozen LLM Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21227)
*Kyupaeck Jeff Rah,Midum Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM的两层框架，用于在力约束下实现精密装配任务，LLM负责分配力上限和选择恢复策略，底层控制器执行力约束，在易碎物体放置和0.4mm间隙齿轮插入任务上实现无损坏完成，在滑脱故障恢复中优于强按策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件强化学习虽能实现精密装配，但实际部署需要为每个对象确定合适的力上限，并在插入失败时在不超过该上限的前提下进行恢复

Method: 两层框架：冻结的文本LLM在执行前为每个对象分配力上限，并使用紧凑文本力签名从固定动作菜单中选择恢复动作；低层控制器强制执行力上限，恢复策略不能增加上限，隐藏的断裂阈值仅评估器知晓

Result: 单一策略在256次评估中成功完成易碎和鲁棒对象任务且无损坏，正确预测释放时机，完整拾取插入流程平均峰值力5.4N；在注入握持滑脱情况下，力签名恢复策略在两个夹爪上分别解决40%和64%的故障，而强按基准要么无效要么导致频繁损坏；PPO在严格力约束下无法解决问题，学习到的释放策略也失败

Conclusion: 该框架在力约束装配任务中有效，优于基准方法，尤其在故障恢复场景中表现突出

Abstract: Force-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) enables tight-clearance assembly under a commanded force ceiling, but practical deployment requires determining an appropriate force limit for each object and recovering from insertion failures without exceeding it. We present a two-layer framework in which a frozen, text-only large language model (LLM) assigns a per-object force ceiling before execution and selects recovery maneuvers from a fixed action menu using compact textual force signatures. The LLM never controls force directly: a low-level controller enforces the force ceiling, the recovery policy cannot increase it, and the hidden breaking-force threshold is known only to the evaluator.
  We evaluate the framework on fragile bottle placement and 0.4 mm diametral-clearance gear insertion using two grippers (Robotiq 2F-140 and Franka Panda hand). A single policy passes 256/256 evaluation episodes on both fragile and robust objects without breakage, correctly predicts release timing, and completes a full table-pick-and-insert pipeline with a mean peak force of 5.4 N. Under injected in-grip slip, the force-signature recovery strategy resolves 40% and 64% of failures on the two grippers, whereas a press-harder baseline is either ineffective or causes frequent breakage. We also report negative results, including the failure of PPO to solve the task under strict force constraints and unsuccessful learned release strategies. All experiments are conducted in rigid-body simulation with hidden force-threshold breakage; no sim-to-real claim is made.

</details>


### [51] [GS-Agent: Creating 4D Physical Worlds With Generative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21522)
*Hongxin Zhang,Chunru Lin,Junyan Li,Zhou Xian,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Agent是一个端到端多智能体框架，利用基础模型和物理引擎，将自然语言描述转化为具有丰富物理交互和电影级摄像机控制的动态4D物理世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建动态且物理真实的4D世界具有挑战性：传统方法依赖手工制作，需要大量人力；虽然生成式基础模型展现出潜力，但仍难以确保物理合理性和可控性；需要一种能够自动化整个4D世界创建过程的方案。

Method: GS-Agent采用多智能体架构，将任务分解为实体管理（3D资产策展、材质调优、放置、运动控制）和渲染配置（摄像机和光照操控），多个专业智能体通过代码与物理引擎交互，寻求多模态反馈并协作迭代构建4D世界。

Result: 实验表明GS-Agent能将自然语言转化为多样化且物理合理的4D世界，实现液体、可变形物体和刚体之间的丰富交互，并达到电影级摄像机和光照控制效果。

Conclusion: GS-Agent为4D世界生成的新范式奠定基础，赋能创意内容创作和物理AI，项目页面为 https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/gs-agent/。

Abstract: Creating dynamic and physically realistic 4D worlds from natural language descriptions is both fascinating and challenging. Traditional computer graphics methods rely on manual creation, requiring extensive human effort to fine-tune materials, motions, and visual fidelity. Recent advances in generative foundation models have sparked interest in learning to generate such 4D worlds from large-scale data; however, existing methods still struggle to ensure physical plausibility and controllability. In this work, we take a different path by leveraging foundation models to construct an agentic system that emulates how humans traditionally create 4D worlds, yet automates the entire process. We present GS-Agent, an end-to-end multi-agent framework that integrates physics engines in the loop to generate realistic, dynamic, and controllable 4D physical worlds from natural language. Inspired by how humans build 4D worlds, GS-Agent decomposes the task into entity management, covering 3D asset curation, material tuning, placement, and motion control, and rendering configuration, including camera and lighting manipulation. Multiple agents with distinct expertise interact with the physics engine via code, seek multimodal feedback, and collaborate to iteratively construct 4D worlds that align with the given descriptions. Experimental results show that GS-Agent effectively converts natural language into diverse and physically plausible 4D worlds exhibiting rich interactions among liquids, deformable objects, and rigid bodies, while achieving cinematic camera and lighting control. We envision GS-Agent as a foundation for a new paradigm in 4D world generation, empowering creative content creation and physical AI. Project page at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/gs-agent/

</details>

### [52] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AINTMA是一个多智能体AI系统，将传统测试管理转化为自主质量智能生态系统，包含6个专用智能体，在分布式云环境中实现自适应决策，测试优先级准确率达88.4%，测试周期时间减少43%，缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%，9个月收回成本并获得340%投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要智能、自主的系统，能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策，以应对日益复杂的软件测试管理挑战。

Method: AINTMA采用云原生微服务基础设施，部署6个专用AI智能体：测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能和云安全监控。通过零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT认证、加密代理间通信和多租户隔离实现安全多智能体协调。生成式质量智能智能体使用大语言模型生成自然语言质量报告，强化学习智能体将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，从大规模历史执行数据（47个特征，36个月滚动窗口）中学习上下文策略。

Result: 在12个异构软件项目上18个月评估显示：测试优先级准确率88.4%（vs 随机51.2%、最佳商业基准82.1%）；测试周期时间减少43%；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；9个月收回成本并获得340%投资回报率。该架构可扩展至50,000+测试用例，响应时间小于400ms，生成式智能模块获得4.3/5.0开发者有用性评分。

Conclusion: 结合自主多智能体协调、生成式智能和安全智能连接的代理AI，能够从根本上推动云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理进步，证明代理AI架构在提升测试效率和质量保证方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [53] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次对LLM水印在医疗领域的严格评估，发现水印可能导致词汇损坏、幻觉术语和图像发现错误归属等临床相关失败，强调领域特定评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在临床工作流程中的广泛应用需要可靠的输出追踪（水印），但现有水印评估主要针对通用基准，医疗领域（微小token扰动可能显著改变语义）的评估不足。

Method: 在11个LLM和7个VLM上测试5种水印方案；涵盖单模态和多模态临床推理任务；引入人类专家验证的管道，系统审计医疗推理质量、术语精确性和诱导幻觉。

Result: 水印导致多种失败模式下的实质性降级：词汇损坏、幻觉术语、放大图像发现的错误归属或遗漏；缺乏领域分析+聚合指标会系统掩盖实际降级。

Conclusion: 在医疗领域部署带水印的模型前必须进行领域特定评估，当前基准测试可能掩盖临床上重要的失败。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [54] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了自一致性(Self-consistency)与多样化集成在语言模型不确定性估计中的表现。通过Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验，研究发现单模型自一致性虽能提供准确的逐题不确定性估计，但无法揭示跨题结构；而多样化集成则能检测到模型未知信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型重复运行时的输出差异是否反映其未知信息，并比较自一致性与多样化集成在揭示模型不确定性方面的有效性。

Method: 在相同问题上对比两种设置：单模型运行100次(τ=1)与24个LLM的集成运行一次(τ=0)。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验分离信号与采样噪声，在5个数据集家族和3个基准(MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K)上进行测试。

Result: 单模型内部最多1个维度超过噪声阈值；集成中4个特征值超过噪声阈值，而匹配难度伯努利零假设下500次蒙特卡洛抽样的最大值为1。自一致性提供准确的逐题不确定性但无跨题结构；多样化集成能揭示模型未知信息。

Conclusion: 自一致性适合逐题不确定性估计但不具备跨题结构检测能力；只有多样化集成能有效发现模型未知的领域，说明模型内部一致性无法替代多样化集成。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [55] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一种无需训练的框架，通过动态连续验证策略和草稿引导解码机制，有效克服了JPDE问题，使扩散大语言模型在中等置信度区域能够实现可靠加速，最高可实现53.19倍（长序列）至105.02倍（结合KV-Cache）的推理速度提升，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的并行解码是提高推理效率的关键，但当前策略因JPDE（联合概率依赖误差）需要过于保守的置信度阈值，导致冗余的去噪迭代和次优的推理速度。

Method: DC-Leap提出两个核心机制：1）动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束集成到并行解码过程中，通过逐步验证token依赖关系来中和JPDE；2）草稿引导解码机制，利用草稿token向前跳跃多token，提供前瞻上下文并保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，DC-Leap实现了显著加速：在MBPP数据集长序列生成上达到53.19倍加速，与KV-Cache结合时最高达105.02倍加速，且生成质量保持相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap是一种有效的训练-free框架，能够在中等置信度区域内可靠地加速dLLMs，通过动态连续验证和草稿引导解码机制解决了JPDE问题，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [56] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench是一个用于评估AI代理在开放式AI工程环境中优化大语言模型推理速度能力的基准测试。与依赖已知配方和超参数调优的现有基准不同，该任务要求代理部署兼容OpenAI的推理服务器并优化推理速度。15种前沿代理配置在优化表现上显著优于朴素PyTorch基线（最高提升8.08倍），并能匹配或超越默认设置的服务引擎（vLLM提升4.05倍），但仍低于同一时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高提升11.53倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常在预设工作流程或狭窄动作空间上评估AI代理。即使名义上开放式的任务，往往只需检索已知配方和调整少量超参数即可解决，这使得强结果是否反映真正的优化还是记忆的解决方案变得不清楚。研究需要一个新的基准来评估代理在开放式的AI工程设置中的优化能力。

Method: 提出InferenceBench基准测试，要求代理部署OpenAI兼容的推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个代理接收目标LLM、一张H100 GPU、一个优化场景和2小时的墙上时钟时间预算。设计了四种优化场景：前缀延迟、解码延迟、并发请求吞吐量以及平衡所有三个指标的混合场景。评估了15种前沿代理配置的表现。

Result: 15种代理配置显著优于朴素PyTorch基线（最高提升8.08倍），并能匹配或超越默认设置的服务引擎（vLLM提升4.05倍），但仍低于同一时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高提升11.53倍）。定性分析显示，尽管代理枚举了许多相关优化技术，但它们 overwhelmingly 收敛于单个推理框架。它们只测试少数不同配置，并将剩余预算用于重新测量、修复或优化超参数，而不是探索实质不同的策略。

Conclusion: 瓶颈不在于领域知识，而在于提出多样化配置、系统评估它们并提交最佳识别解决方案的能力。InferenceBench反映了代理在开放式AI工程设置中操作的能力，其中记忆的解决方案会导致改进有限。该基准测试揭示了当前代理在开放优化任务中的局限性和改进方向。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [57] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DecodeShare 协议，识别解码时隐藏状态中跨任务共享的低维子空间，并证明其具有因果重要性及实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使用单一参数集处理多种任务，但在 KV 缓存推理中，解码阶段与预填充阶段相比，其任务通用的结构尚不明确；此外，在激活引导中如何可靠地利用解码时信号也缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 提出 DecodeShare 协议：在解码时的隐藏状态中识别跨任务一致共享的低维子空间，并通过仅在解码阶段移除该子空间来检验其因果作用；同时对比预填充派生子空间和随机子空间。

Result: 移除该共享子空间导致的性能下降远大于移除预填充派生或随机子空间；该共享子空间与常见的激活引导方向存在重叠，直接影响任务通用解码通道；通过直接投影移除该子空间可分离各组件的功能角色，且在解码时评估引导向量比基于预填充的代理更能提供可靠信号。

Conclusion: 尽管共享子空间紧凑，但在解码阶段仍可作为高杠杆的因果通道；该方法为理解与利用解码时隐藏状态提供了新的视角与实用手段。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [58] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE框架通过数据分解、指令微调和提示推理构建任务特定LLM，DTI规划器优化基础LLM选择，提升构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强LLM任务特定能力需要大量指令微调数据，但存在高昂标注成本且缺乏针对性优化方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架（数据分解、指令微调、提示推理）和DTI规划器，用于选择最优基础LLM及DTI组合。

Result: 实验验证PlanE有效性：跨数据集同基础LLM和同数据集异基础LLM两种场景均表现良好；DTI规划器在不同优化目标下具备泛化性。

Conclusion: PlanE框架与DTI规划器可有效降低构建任务特定LLM的成本并提升效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [59] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCGS通过在对话的每一轮推断用户意图来提升LLM安全性，在多个基准测试中表现优于强基线和前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话中区分用户恶意攻击与善意误解是LLM安全的核心挑战，现有框架采用上下文带子方法忽略了对话轨迹，导致模型要么过于谨慎伤害合法用户，要么过于宽松易被利用。

Method: 将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在词元和话语两个层级学习基于价值和后悔的批评者，通过动作值函数对候选响应进行评分，利用推理时重加权近似基策略的指数倾斜，确保在有限候选池中提升期望回报。

Result: 在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench和Harmbench数据集上，DCGS在对抗性对话任务中优于强鲁棒基线和前沿模型；且该方法可迁移到前沿模型，无需微调即可提升其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DCGS通过全程推断用户意图并动态调整响应策略，有效缓解了恶意与善意意图的歧义问题，为多轮对话下的LLM安全提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [60] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler是一个统一、tile感知的Triton内核套件，用于垂直融合LLM推理中的完整采样管道，支持动态请求行为和CUDA Graph执行，通过创新的分层两级top-k算法实现高达10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM采样实现存在三大局限：1）仅加速管道子集；2）依赖多次内核启动；3）假设批次中采样行为一致，限制了动态服务负载支持和CUDA Graph执行效率。

Method: 提出SonicSampler，采用tile-aware Triton内核垂直融合完整采样管道；核心是新颖的分层两级top-k算法，利用LLM输出的低熵结构实现高效词汇表选择；支持语法约束解码、重复/频率/存在惩罚、logit偏置、温度缩放、top-k/top-p/min-p过滤及推测验证等多种采样行为。

Result: 在竞争基准上实现高达10倍加速；在异构推测解码负载中相比最先进基准实现16倍加速；在保持灵活批次执行的同时完全兼容CUDA Graph。

Conclusion: SonicSampler通过统一的工作负载感知执行模型和创新的算法设计，解决了现有采样实现的多重瓶颈，为LLM推理的高效动态服务提供了关键优化方案。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [61] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Wasserstein距离的统一半监督框架\algo{}，通过图-文本协作编码模块和双路径编码器，解决文本属性图在LLM场景下的可扩展性问题，同时生成人类可读的文本属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAG表示学习方法在扩展性上存在瓶颈，与LLMs结合时尤为突出；现有数据蒸馏方法无法捕获图-文本模态的复杂交互，难以处理半监督场景下的标签稀缺问题，且缺乏生成人类可读文本属性的能力。

Method: 1) 基于Wasserstein距离（WSD）的统一半监督框架；2) 图-文本协作编码模块，采用双路径编码器（图感知和图自由）；3) 协作自训练方案，用于获取可靠伪标签和融合互补特征；4) 基于WSD的图草图算法；5) 基于聚类的关键词提取LLM文本合成模块，生成连贯、人类可读的摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上实验表明，\algo{}在GNN和LLM下游任务中均实现了最先进的性能-压缩权衡，能够有效且高效地进行TAG学习或分析。

Conclusion: \algo{}成功解决了TAG表示学习的可扩展性瓶颈，通过创新的协作编码和WSD理论，实现了高性能与低成本的平衡，为下游LLM任务提供了高质量的人类可读文本属性。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统探究了影响大语言模型欺骗输出检测性能的关键因素，包括表示深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示以及欺骗类型学。通过引入包含伪造、省略和夸大等多样化欺骗类型的数据集，对七种探针进行了对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，欺骗检测探针在跨域场景下表现不佳，即在一种类型欺骗上训练的模型难以迁移到其他类型的欺骗检测中。因此需要系统研究各种因素如何影响检测性能。

Method: 在标准基准训练数据基础上，增加了包含伪造、省略和夸大等多样化欺骗类型的补充数据集。在表示深度、探针表达能力、稀疏特征表示和欺骗类型学四个维度，对七种探针进行了对比分析。

Result: 最优表示深度高度依赖于数据集；更具表达能力的探针相比线性基线仅获得选择性提升；稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态表现相似；训练数据和欺骗类型的选择显著改变可检测性。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度依赖于表示表示的问题，训练数据和欺骗类型的选择会实质性改变检测效果，表明在构建检测系统时需要特别关注表示表示的选择和训练数据的多样性。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [63] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO是一种无训练的本地-云端协作框架，通过利用本地模型在推理时的响应一致性信号来动态决策执行任务的位置（本地或云端），无需额外的路由器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地-云端协作方法通常依赖训练好的路由器或协作感知微调，但这些方法将路由行为与特定运行环境绑定，训练成本高且适应性有限。论文提出响应一致性信号本身已足够用于决策，无需额外训练。

Method: CARGO框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 提示变化的采样以估计本地模型的响应一致性；(2) 贝叶斯早停机制实现样本高效的不确定性控制；(3) 轻量级部署时校准以支持任意目标协作比率。整个框架无需训练任何参数。

Result: 在多种推理和问答任务中，多个LLM家族和规模，以及预训练/微调的本地模型上，CARGO一致优于其他无训练基线，在若干设置中甚至超过监督学习的路由器。

Conclusion: 有效的、适应性强的本地-云端协作可以直接从本地模型的内在响应行为中涌现，无需额外的训练路由器，为资源受限环境下的LLM部署提供了更简单、更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [64] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaTrail是一个针对个性化网页代理的基准测试，结合PACMem框架，利用真实浏览轨迹评估代理从历史中推断用户偏好和回忆信息的能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试要么限制任务为完全明确的提示，要么将网页交互历史抽象为简化形式，无法捕捉个性化任务，导致个性化网页代理能力评估存在空白

Method: 提出PersonaTrail基准测试，采用真实浏览轨迹作为用户历史；设计PACMem框架，将原始浏览历史分解为事实记忆（总结单个会话）和偏好记忆（提炼重复行为模式），推理时检索相关记忆指导个性化导航

Result: 在PersonaTrail基准上，PACMem框架在各类任务中持续优于现有的基于记忆的基线方法

Conclusion: PersonaTrail与PACMem有效填补了个性化网页代理评估的空白，证明利用结构化记忆处理原始浏览历史可显著提升代理的个性化导航能力

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [65] [Expectation Alignment of Language Models for Real-World User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20485)
*Miaomiao Li,Yang Wang,Bin Liang,Shudong Liu,Zhiwei Zhang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ExpectBench基准和LENS框架，通过提取真实用户期望来提升LLM的期望满足能力，揭示模型与用户期望的根本错位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法（启发式、专家标准、用户模拟）无法捕捉真实用户期望的多样性和微妙性，导致模型在基准上表现良好但与用户实际需求错位

Method: 1. 提出从真实用户交互中提取语义丰富期望的流程；2. 构建ExpectBench基准；3. 提出LENS轻量级潜在期望感知响应生成框架

Result: 当前LLM难以满足和预测用户期望；LENS能显著提升期望满足率，证明显式建模用户期望对对齐的重要性

Conclusion: 显式建模用户期望是实现真实人机对齐的关键，当前LLM存在根本性期望错位问题

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on standard benchmarks, yet it remains largely unexplored whether they truly meet user expectations. Existing evaluation approaches, relying on model heuristics, expert rubrics, or user simulation, fail to capture the diversity and subtlety of real human expectations, causing models to appear competent while misaligning with what users actually seek. We present the first systematic study of user expectations in real-world LLM interactions, proposing a principled procedure to extract semantically rich expectations and introducing ExpectBench, a benchmark grounded in real user expectations. Analyses reveal that current LLMs struggle to satisfy and anticipate what users hope to obtain, highlighting a fundamental source of misalignment. Building on these observations, we propose LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework. LENS enables models to internalize user expectations and generate better-aligned responses, consistently improving expectation satisfaction and underscoring the importance of explicitly modeling user expectations for realistic human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [66] [Autonomous Topology Mutation: Safe Runtime Restructuring for Multi-Agent LLM Systems with Capability, State, and Shadow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20488)
*Bronislav Sidik,Chaya Levi,Nizzan Kimhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATM是一种运行时团队突变机制，通过监控六信号瓶颈指数并应用三项安全不变量，当检测到代理过载时将其分解为专业化子代理，从而动态重构多智能体LLM框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM框架通常在启动时固定团队拓扑结构，但当单个代理在运行时因混合过多动作类别、工具错误累积或排队等待过多调用而过载时，系统缺乏自我重构的机制。

Method: ATM结合遥测驱动的过载检测与三项安全不变量（能力单调性、状态路由完备性、影子前验证）作为结构变更的守门条件。它监控包含队列深度、上下文混乱、工具错误率、角色熵、重试循环率和跨代理等待时间的六信号瓶颈指数；当超过预热校准阈值多tick时，将过载代理分解为专业化子代理，热交换父代理至协调者角色，并通过隐私级感知路由控制状态转移（每个记忆原子仅路由至允许的子集或明确丢弃并记录原因），在通过影子验证窗口前不接收实时流量。

Result: 在720个DeepSeek-V3驱动的任务运行中，ATM分解器将代码任务成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%；完整铁路与蒸馏系统在正则分类器下将检测到的高隐私内存暴露从2.0降至0.0事件/任务，同时保持任务质量；运行时铁路（携带ATM不变量）在代理热路径上增加不到500微秒的p99延迟。

Conclusion: 该实现、基准测试套件和追踪数据已开源，包含一个使用真实Python执行的小型实时工具探针作为外部有效性检查。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM frameworks typically fix their team topology at boot time. When an individual agent becomes overloaded at runtime, for example by mixing too many action categories, accumulating tool errors, or queueing behind too many calls, the system has no mechanism to restructure itself. We introduce Autonomous Topology Mutation (ATM), a runtime team-mutation mechanism for multi-agent LLM frameworks. ATM combines telemetry-driven overload detection with three safety invariants that gate each structural change: capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, and shadow-before-live validation.
  ATM monitors a six-signal Bottleneck Index that includes queue depth, context thrash, tool-error rate, role entropy, retry-loop rate, and cross-agent wait time. When a warmup-calibrated threshold is breached for multiple consecutive ticks, ATM factorises the overloaded agent into specialised sub-agents and hot-swaps the parent into a coordinator role while preserving its external identity. State transfer is controlled by privacy-level-aware routing: each memory atom is routed only to a permitted child set, or explicitly dropped with a logged reason. No candidate topology receives live traffic until it has passed a shadow validation window.
  On 720 DeepSeek-V3-driven task runs with deterministic tool stubs across four ablation conditions and three workloads, the ATM factoriser split lifts code-task success from 3.3% to 61.7%. The full rail-and-distillation system reduces detected high-privacy memory exposure under a regex classifier from 2.0 to 0.0 events per task while preserving task quality. The runtime rails carrying ATM's invariants add less than 500 microseconds of p99 latency on the agent hot path. A small live-tool probe with real Python execution is included as an external-validity check. The implementation, benchmark harness, and traces are open-sourced.

</details>


### [67] [EvoSQL: Memory-Augmented Critic-Generator Co-Evolution for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20489)
*Jiawei Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Chenyu Zhou,Chaojian Shi,Ming Dong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoSQL是一种基于记忆的协同进化框架，通过生成器和批评者之间的迭代交互来改进复杂SQL查询的Text-to-SQL生成，引入执行感知的监督进行自我蒸馏策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂数据库查询需要超越单次生成的推理能力，包括多步分解、执行诊断和针对性修正。现有方法难以处理复杂SQL生成任务。

Method: 1. 协同进化框架：生成器与批评者迭代交互；2. 上下文候选记忆；3. 双重验证（执行信号+LLM批评）；4. 基于效用引导的聚合更新记忆；5. 自我蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）微调阶段，将执行感知的监督注入现代编码LLM骨干网络。

Result: 在Spider和BIRD数据集上，EvoSQL相比Maj@16基线持续提升开源模型性能，BIRD-Dev上提升显著（Qwen3-4B +1.37%，Qwen2.5-Coder-3B +9.19%）；SDPO初始化进一步提升了Spider-Test和BIRD-Dev性能。

Conclusion: 基于记忆的协同进化是实现更可靠和可推广的Text-to-SQL系统的有效路径。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL has advanced rapidly with large language models, but complex database queries still require reasoning beyond one-shot generation, including multi-step decomposition, execution-based diagnosis, and targeted correction. We present EvoSQL, a co-evolution framework that formulates SQL synthesis as an iterative interaction between a generator and a critic. EvoSQL maintains a contextualized candidate memory, verifies SQL candidates with both execution signals and LLM-based critique, and updates its memory through utility-guided aggregation. To strengthen the underlying generator-critic pair, we further introduce a Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) fine-tuning stage that injects execution-aware supervision into modern coding LLM backbones. Experiments on Spider and BIRD show that EvoSQL consistently improves open-source models over Maj@16 baselines, with particularly large gains on BIRD-Dev, ranging from +1.37% for Qwen3-4B to +9.19% for Qwen2.5-Coder-3B. SDPO initialization further improves selected backbones on Spider-Test and BIRD-Dev. These results suggest that memory-grounded co-evolution is an effective path toward more reliable and generalizable Text-to-SQL systems. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/EvoSQL.

</details>


### [68] [From Errors to Rules: Iterative Prompt Optimization for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20497)
*Yueying Cui,Renhao Xue,Yi Zhang,Mukul Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了文本分类提示优化的多种范式，提出了一种新的错误引导优化方法ERGO，该方法在边界可学习任务上表现优异，并提供了任务特征与范式选择的互补性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化方法（如演示选择、探索式搜索、错误驱动诊断）具有已知的优缺点，但这些优缺点尚未被充分刻画。需要理解不同范式在不同任务类型上的表现，以指导实际应用中的方法选择。

Method: 作者首先对多种提示优化范式进行了全面的实证研究，然后提出错误引导优化（ERGO）方法。ERGO在非重叠批次中迭代遍历整个训练集，诊断分类错误，并通过诊断-处方-重写的反馈循环生成有针对性的决策规则。

Result: ERGO在边界可学习任务上达到最高准确率（TREC: 90.0%, CLINC150: 94.4%），3-5次迭代收敛，并能生成可解释的决策规则。不同范式各有优势：基于演示的ICL在覆盖率依赖任务上表现最好，探索式搜索在多类意图任务上表现最好，ERGO在可从错误模式学习的边界可学习任务上表现最好。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个互补性框架，将任务特征与最优范式选择联系起来，为从业者提供了实用的指导原则，表明没有单一方法能统治所有任务类型。

Abstract: Prompt optimization for text classification spans diverse approaches, from demonstration selection to exploration-based search to error-driven diagnosis, each with known but incompletely characterized strengths and limitations. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study across diverse classification benchmarks (2 to 150 classes) comparing these paradigms through both quantitative evaluation and qualitative analysis of optimization traces, revealing that each paradigm excels on structurally different task types and that no single method dominates. Guided by these insights, we propose Error-Guided Optimization (ERGO), an error-driven method that iterates over the full training set in non-overlapping batches, diagnoses classification failures, and generates targeted decision rules through a diagnose-prescribe-rewrite feedback loop. ERGO achieves the best accuracy on tasks where errors concentrate in specific confused label pairs (which we term boundary-learnable tasks): TREC: 90.0%, CLINC150: 94.4%, converges in 3-5 iterations, and produces interpretable decision rules. While ERGO does not achieve the highest overall average, it fills a complementary role: demonstration-based ICL wins on coverage-dependent tasks, exploration-based search wins on many-class intent, and ERGO wins where decision boundaries are learnable from error patterns. We provide a complementarity framework linking task characteristics to optimal paradigm selection, offering practical guidance for practitioners.

</details>


### [69] [AISE-Bench: A Full-Cycle Curated Benchmark for Information Seeking on Academic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20498)
*Fanjin Zhang,Zhengyang Wang,Ruixuan Huang,Kefan Zhang,Amy Xin,Yuanchun Wang,Shu Zhao,Evgeny Kharlamov,Jie Tang,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AISE-Bench是一个面向学术知识图谱信息检索的真实世界全流程标注基准，包含1,133个问答对、API执行轨迹、验证参数和带引用的答案，用于评估多步骤API使用LLM智能体的逐步正确性、推理可追溯性和 grounded 总结能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用基准存在局限性：依赖合成模板、简化解空间或狭窄任务（如论文中心任务），未充分探索真实用户意图、复杂多步骤API规划、丰富参数填充、带引用的grounded答案以及过程与结果的综合评估等关键挑战。

Method: 构建AISE-Bench基准：包含1,133个问答对及查询分类、完整API执行轨迹、验证参数、带引用的grounded答案；设计定制化标注工作流支持规划、执行与迭代；开发综合评估协议，涵盖答案质量、引用grounding、API规划正确性和执行成功率。

Result: 在14个评估方法中，最强模型PLAY2PROMPT（Gemini-3-Pro）仅达到中等水平，常在API规划和执行中遇到困难，表明当前LLM智能体在复杂多步骤API使用任务上仍存在显著性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: AISE-Bench为量化评估和改进多步骤API使用LLM智能体的逐步正确性、grounded总结与可追溯推理提供了具有挑战性的新测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) augmented with tools are emerging as autonomous agents capable of using Web engine, APIs, and code to solve complex, long-horizon tasks. Current tool-using benchmarks for information seeking on academic graphs rely on synthetic templates, simplified solution spaces, or narrow tasks such as paper-centric tasks, leaving key challenges underexplored - realistic user intent, complex multi-step API planning, rich parameter filling for APIs, grounded answers with references, and comprehensive evaluation of both the process and the outcome. We introduce AISE-Bench, a real-world, full-cycle annotated benchmark for information seeking on academic knowledge graphs. AISE-Bench release contains 1,133 QA pairs, including query taxonomies, full API execution trajectories, validated parameters, and source-grounded answers with reference links. To support high-quality annotation, we design a customized agent workflow to enable annotators to plan, execute, and revise complex API workflows efficiently. We develop a comprehensive evaluation protocol measuring answer quality, reference grounding, API-planning correctness, and execution success. Among the 14 evaluated methods, even the strongest model (PLAY2PROMPT with Gemini-3-Pro) achieves only moderate performance and often struggles with API planning and execution. AISE-Bench establishes a challenging new testbed for quantitatively evaluating and improving the stepwise correctness, grounded summarization, and traceable reasoning of multi-step API-using LLM agents. Our code and data are available at https://aise-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [70] [FlowEdit: Information-Theoretic Control of LLM Reasoning Flows for Ill-posed Problems Involving Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20500)
*Sizhe Tang,Guangyu Jiang,Yu Li,Rongqian Chen,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit 是一种基于信息论的 LLM 框架，通过调节内部推理流来处理涉及冲突的病构问题，使其能够生成一组多样化的替代响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 在处理涉及冲突、不一致或互斥条件的病构问题时表现不佳。传统的下一个词预测机制难以维持多个竞争假设并生成替代响应。需要让模型能够显式处理冲突，在单次推理中生成多样化的替代方案。

Method: FlowEdit 框架利用信息论原理量化并调节 LLM 的内部推理流。它通过两种双重的信息论目标实现分支感知的推理过程：最大化每个选定假设到分支结果的信信息流，同时最小化兄弟分支间的重叠和条件依赖。该框架通过边界嵌入为 ε-充分时在可处理的变分界下优化底层条件互信息。

Result: 在大量实验中，FlowEdit 超越了领先的专有模型，将精确集合匹配准确率提高了 68%，同时将整体响应的信息丰富度提升了 24%。研究还表明，推理流在 token 序列中表现为下一个词熵的重分配：熵在每个分支内集中，在流边界处放大，并随所需流数量缩放。

Conclusion: 通过调节推理流，LLMs 可以在单次推理中生成涵盖广泛、信息丰富的替代响应，有效处理病构问题中的冲突。这种分支感知的推理机制为 LLM 在开放世界中的鲁棒性提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on well-specified reasoning tasks with a feasible answer. However, problems encountered in the open world can become ill-posed due to inconsistent conditions, conflicting statements, or mutually incompatible requirements, admitting no valid responses. We argue that reasoning of such ill-posed problems involving conflicts require novel LLM capabilities to make hidden conflicts explicit, maintain competing hypotheses via multiple reasoning branches, and generate alternative responses in a single pass, all of which are challenging due to the limitation of the next-token prediction mechanism in LLMs. To this end, we propose FlowEdit, a novel framework that leverages information-theoretic principles to quantify and regulate internal reasoning flows of LLMs, for generating a full set of alternative responses under valid hypotheses. FlowEdit can be viewed as enforcing a branch-aware reasoning process using two dual information-theoretic objectives on the model's internal reasoning representations: maximizing the information flow from each selected hypothesis to the branch outcome, while minimizing the overlap and conditional dependence across sibling branches, to provide a diverse, informative set of responses with broad coverage. We show that this is achieved through tractable variational bounds under boundary embeddings being ε-sufficient, optimizing the underlying conditional mutual information in LLM reasoning process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowEdit outperforms leading proprietary models, improving exact-set-match accuracy by 68%, while boosting overall response informativeness by 24%. We further show that flow regulation surfaces in the token stream as a redistribution of next-token entropy that concentrates inside each branch, amplifies at flow boundaries, and scales with the number of flows the problem requires.

</details>


### [71] [MKEvolve: A Modular Multi-Agent Framework for Kernel Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20501)
*Jason Yoo,Rajarshi Saha,Shaowei Zhu,Tao Yu,Wei Tang,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKEvolve是一个迭代式框架，通过共同演化PyTorch模块的模块化分解与LLM生成的子内核，优化硬件加速器的内核编写，在KernelBench测试中提升了正确性和性能，同时降低35%的LLM令牌使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在代码生成方面取得进展，但为硬件加速器编写正确且高性能的内核仍然是扩展现代ML工作负载的关键瓶颈。

Method: MKEvolve通过迭代式共同演化PyTorch模块的模块化分解与LLM生成的子内核，在迭代中通过分裂和融合优化分解结构，并利用LLM驱动的束搜索独立改进每个子内核。

Result: 生成的内核由独立验证的子内核组成，具备可配置性（子内核可交换）、可解释性（错误和加速可追溯至特定子内核）和可适配性（易于适配相关模型架构）。

Conclusion: 在Triton和KernelBench L2/L3上的实验表明，MKEvolve在提升正确性和加速性能的同时，相比端到端直接合成基线将LLM令牌使用量降低了最多35%。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in LLM-based code generation, writing correct and performant kernels for hardware accelerators remains a key bottleneck in scaling modern ML workloads. We present MKEvolve (Modular Kernel Evolve), a framework that iteratively co-evolves a modular decomposition of complex PyTorch modules and the LLM-generated kernel for each submodule, refining the decomposition by splitting and fusing across iterations while independently improving each subkernel via LLM-driven beam search. The resulting kernels are programmatic compositions of independently verified subkernels, making them configurable (subkernel implementations are swappable), interpretable (errors and speedups are traceable to specific subkernels), and readily adaptable to related model architectures. Experiments with Triton on KernelBench L2 and L3, spanning multi-operator sequences and full model architectures, show that MKEvolve improves both correctness and speedup over end-to-end direct synthesis baselines while reducing LLM token usage by up to 35%.

</details>


### [72] [LeanFlow: A Case Study in Workflow-Driven Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20503)
*Lazar Milikic,Simon Guilloud,Khanh Nguyen,Viktor Kuncak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanFlow是一个专门用于将数学论文转化为可构建Lean项目的LLM代理系统。研究通过案例研究评估了不同运行时机制对文档到项目形式化过程中的完成度、可审计性和效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于验证器的系统已能生成大型形式化工件，但文档到项目形式化过程中哪些运行时机制会影响完成度、可审计性和效率尚不清楚。

Method: 通过在两个未形式化的数论和测度论论文上的案例研究，使用模型、证明工作流和工具集的消融实验，在Kimi2.6和GPT5.5上进行评估，报告了任务结果、API调用、输入和输出token数。

Result: Kimi2.6完整工作流在2000次调用预算内完成两个文档级项目，无队列变体达到预算上限；GPT5.5所有文档级变体均完成，完整工作流在两个源上具有最低或并列最低的输入token成本。LeanFlow在RLM25的PFR切片上达到75.7%的BEq+，并在GPT5.5运行中解决了所有五个ICML 2026 AI for Math TCS挑战项目。

Conclusion: LeanFlow是一个有效的系统，能够成功将数学论文转化为形式化项目，在案例研究和基准测试中均表现出良好的性能。

Abstract: We present and evaluate LeanFlow, an LLM agent system specialized for translating mathematical papers into buildable Lean projects. Recent verifier-in-the-loop systems show that large formal artifacts can be produced, but it remains unclear which runtime mechanisms affect completion, auditability, or efficiency in document-to-project formalization. We study this question through case studies on two previously unformalized mathematical papers in number theory and measure theory, using model, proof-workflow, and toolset ablations with Kimi2.6 and GPT5.5; we report task outcome, API calls, input tokens, and output tokens. With Kimi2.6, the full workflow completes both document-level projects within the 2000-call budget, while no-queue variants reach the budget limit; with GPT5.5, all document-level variants complete, and the full workflow has the lowest or tied-lowest input-token cost on both sources. As complementary calibration, LeanFlow reaches 75.7% BEq+ on the PFR slice of RLM25 and solves all five ICML 2026 AI for Math TCS challenge projects in our GPT5.5 runs.

</details>


### [73] [Optimizing Hypergraph-Based RAG: Toward Better Fact Extraction and Chunk Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20506)
*Houda Khrouf,Pedro Fillastre,Sebastiao Correia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的HyperGraphRAG方法，通过自一致性提示提升LLM提取准确性，并使用个性化PageRank算法优化超图上的块检索，解决了GraphRAG和HyperGraphRAG在n-ary事实处理、提取错误和检索效率方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG虽然通过图结构知识增强推理能力，但难以处理n-ary事实；HyperGraphRAG虽然使用超图提供更丰富的语义，但依赖易出错的LLM提取且标准块检索效率低下。

Method: 采用自一致性提示技术改进LLM知识提取的准确性，并基于超图实现个性化PageRank算法来优化知识块的检索过程。

Result: 显著提升了HyperGraphRAG的性能，包括更准确的知识提取和更高效的检索机制。

Conclusion: 通过结合自一致性提示和超图个性化PageRank，有效克服了前两种方法的主要缺陷，实现了更准确的深度推理和更高效的检索性能。

Abstract: GraphRAG enables deeper reasoning by structuring knowledge as graphs but struggles with n-ary facts. HyperGraphRAG uses hypergraphs for richer semantics, improving accuracy, yet relies on error-prone LLM extraction and inefficient standard chunk retrieval. We address this by employing self-consistency prompting to improve the extraction, and Personalized PageRank algorithm over hypergraph to enhance chunk retrieval.

</details>


### [74] [MiniCache: Reusable Program Caching with Small Model Interfaces for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20507)
*Jingquan Chen,Jinghua Piao,Jie Feng,Shaogang Hu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniCache是一个可重用程序缓存框架，将PoT程序转换为参数化缓存对象，使用小模型进行语义变量提取和推测起草，减少大模型的推理成本，在并行服务下可将延迟降低最多3.1倍，吞吐量提高最多2.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在程序辅助推理、智能体决策和结构化任务执行中应用广泛，但这些应用通常具有很高的推理成本。

Method: MiniCache将Program-of-Thought (PoT)程序转换为参数化缓存对象，使用小模型在缓存命中请求时进行语义变量提取，在目标LLM生成期间进行推测起草，从而减少昂贵的目标LLM调用。

Result: 在购物风格请求数据集（WebShop）、Formula和CodeTAT-QA上，MiniCache在并行服务下实现最多3.1倍的延迟降低和2.8倍的吞吐量提升，同时保持任务质量。

Conclusion: 小模型最有效的使用方式不是作为大模型的替代品，而是作为轻量级接口模型，以实现可靠且高效的可重用程序缓存。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for program-aided reasoning, agentic decision making, and structured task execution, but these applications often incur high inference cost. We present MiniCache, a reusable program caching framework that transforms Program-of-Thought (PoT) programs into parameterized cache objects, enabling reusable computation across structurally similar requests. MiniCache reuses the same small model for semantic variable extraction on cache-hit requests and speculative drafting during target-LLM generation, reducing expensive target-LLM invocations while preserving task quality. Experiments on shopping-style request datasets, WebShop, Formula, and CodeTAT-QA demonstrate that MiniCache improves the trade-off between inference latency, cache reuse, and accuracy, achieving up to 3.1x lower latency and 2.8x higher throughput under parallel serving. These results show that small models are most effective not as replacements for large models, but as lightweight interface models that enable reliable and efficient reusable program caching.

</details>


### [75] [Telco-GAIA: Bilingual Benchmark for Agents in Telecom Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20510)
*Dmitrii Khizbullin,Zaid Alyafeai,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Nourah AlSultan,Raghad Alshalan,David R. Pugh,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Telco-GAIA 是一个针对电信领域工具使用代理的跨语言多模态基准测试，包含100个人工验证的问答任务，要求在静态网站快照、SQL数据库和外部网络归档等三种异构源上进行多跳推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法缺乏针对企业级代理的严格、可复现的测试环境，特别是在封闭领域场景下，需要构建一个真实的、包含多种数据模态的基准测试。

Method: 构建包含100个人工验证问答任务（中英双语）的基准测试，要求代理在静态网站快照（HTML/图片/PDF）、合成关系型SQL数据库和外部网络归档等三种异构源上进行平均4.2跳的多跳推理。

Result: 最强模型仅能解决71%的任务；在中等成本预算下降至约40%；视觉导向类别的表现最弱，后端平均得分低于30%，说明在文档和图像理解方面仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: Telco-GAIA 提供了一个具有挑战性和可复现的企业代理测试平台，并为构建封闭领域基准测试提供了模板。

Abstract: We introduce Telco-GAIA, a bilingual, multi-modal benchmark for evaluating tool-using agents on the data of a real-world telecommunications operator. Telco-GAIA comprises 100 human-verified question-answering tasks, in English and Arabic, that each demand multi-hop reasoning (4.2 hops on average) over three heterogeneous sources: a static website snapshot (HTML, images, and linked PDFs), a synthetic relational SQL database, and external web archives, spanning text, image, and tabular modalities. The benchmark is delivered as a sandboxed Docker environment and scored by normalized exact string matching, making evaluation objective, deterministic, and reproducible over time without any LLM-as-a-Judge. Evaluating a purpose-built reference agent across twelve commercial and open LLMs, we find Telco-GAIA challenging: even the strongest model solves only 71% of tasks; under a moderate cost budget, this falls to about 40%, and the visually grounded categories remain the weakest, where the average backend scores below 30%, leaving substantial headroom in document and image understanding. Telco-GAIA offers a rigorous, reproducible testbed for enterprise agents and a template for constructing closed-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [76] [SiGMA: Sign-Guided Merging and Adaptation for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20511)
*Keonhee Park,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SiGMA的框架，用于解决多模态持续指令微调中的负干扰问题，通过训练时的符号引导自适应微调和推理时的符号引导合并来减少对先前知识的冲突并保留有用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCIT方法（如Mixture of Experts或扩展合并方法）主要关注灾难性遗忘，但在推理时仍面临负干扰问题，即新学习的更新会覆盖有用的先前知识，导致整体性能下降。

Method: SiGMA框架包含两个核心组件：1）符号引导自适应微调——减少与过去知识的碰撞，以最小漂移学习当前任务，缓解严重遗忘；2）符号引导合并——选择性缩放显著参数以保留和放大有用的任务特定知识。

Result: 在UCIT和DCL基准测试上，SiGMA显著减少了负干扰，并优于现有的最先进MCIT方法。

Conclusion: SiGMA是一个简单有效的框架，通过符号引导的自适应微调和合并机制，有效缓解了多模态持续指令微调中的负干扰问题，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) is crucial for adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to evolving a sequence of downstream tasks. Prior methods mostly utilize Mixture of Experts or expansion merge approach, primarily focusing on catastrophic forgetting, yet they still suffer from negative interference during inference, where newly learned updates overwrite useful prior knowledge and degrade overall performance. To address this, we propose SiGMA (Sign Guided Merging and Adaptation), a simple yet effective framework that mitigates negative interference with two components: sign guided adaptive tuning during training and sign guided merging at inference. Sign guided adaptive tuning reduces collisions with past knowledge and learns the current task with minimal drift, mitigating severe forgetting. Sign guided merging further improves consolidation by selectively scaling salient parameters to preserve and amplify useful task specific knowledge. Experiments on UCIT and DCL benchmarks show that SiGMA significantly reduces negative interference and outperforms state of the art MCIT methods. Our code is available at SiGMA.

</details>


### [77] [Reliability-Aware LLM Alignment from Inconsistent Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20515)
*Jingyi Huang,Ruohan Zong,Yujun Feng,Liran Ma,Lanyu Shang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Reliability-Guided Preference Optimization (RGPO) 通过估计标注者可靠性、推断潜在真实标签以及采用基于标注共识水平的可靠性感知一致性优化，有效解决了 RLHF 中人类标注不一致和主观性的问题，在 LLM 对齐基准测试中表现出优于现有 RLHF 基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 是对齐 LLM 与人类偏好的关键方法，但人类标注具有内在的不一致性和主观性。现有偏好优化框架（如 DPO）将标注分歧大的模糊对与共识对同等对待，迫使模型过度拟合不一致的监督信号，导致次优对齐效果。

Method: RGPO 提出两个核心组件：1) 估计标注者可靠性并从噪声人类反馈中推断潜在真实标签，以识别稳健的偏好；2) 引入可靠性感知一致性优化，根据标注共识水平动态调整训练目标，使模型优先关注高共识监督信号。

Result: 在 LLM 对齐基准测试中的广泛实验表明，RGPO 有效减少了训练数据中的不一致性和噪声，并实现了优于广泛采用的 RLHF 基线的性能。

Conclusion: RGPO 有效缓解了人类反馈不一致和噪声的影响，通过可靠性感知训练策略提升了 LLM 对齐效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is critical for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, its efficacy is often compromised by the inherent inconsistency and subjectivity of human annotations. Existing preference optimization frameworks, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), typically treat ambiguous pairs with high annotator disagreement identically to those with unanimous consensus, forcing models to overfit to inconsistent supervision signals and leading to suboptimal alignment. In this work, we propose Reliability-Guided Preference Optimization (RGPO), a robust framework designed to mitigate the impact of inconsistent human feedback. RGPO estimates annotator reliability and infers latent ground truth labels from noisy human feedback to identify robust preferences. Furthermore, we introduce a reliability-aware consistency optimization that dynamically modulates the training objective based on the consensus level of annotations, ensuring the model prioritizes high-consensus supervision signals. Extensive experiments on LLM alignment benchmarks demonstrate that RGPO effectively reduces inconsistency and noise in training data and achieves superior performance compared to widely adopted RLHF baselines. Our code and configurations are available at https://github.com/GenieHuang/RGPO.

</details>


### [78] [Representation Robustness Under Executable Reasoning Constraints in Large Language Models for Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20520)
*Sagnik Nath,Edith Aurora Graf,Liang Zhang,Diego Zapata-Rivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究了大型语言模型在数学问题解决中的表示鲁棒性，发现模型在不同等价表示形式（如故事、文字方程、符号方程）下表现存在显著差异，即使数学结构相同，模型的表现也会发生翻转和退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究常将表示等价的数学公式视为可互换，混淆推理错误与界面失败。本研究旨在系统性调查LLM在数学问题解决中的表示鲁棒性问题。

Method: 使用精心策划的数学等价问题数据集，系统地改变同一基础问题的表面表示（包括故事问题、文字方程、符号方程、同构改写），评估5个当代LLM在直接答案生成条件下，以及代码增强条件下（将推理外部化为可执行Python代码）的表现。

Result: 发现显著的表示敏感性：模型在不同等价表示间频繁改变正确性，存在显著翻转率；同构改写下出现系统性退化；代码增强揭示某些模型具有强大潜在推理能力但表现不佳；即使代码执行成功，表示敏感性仍普遍存在。

Conclusion: 推理框架不能消除表示脆弱性，反而暴露了正确性、可靠性、延迟和成本之间的新权衡。表示应被视为LLM评估和部署中的首要接口设计变量，特别是对于AI辅助问题解决系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on mathematical problem solving, yet prior work often treats representationally equivalent formulations as interchangeable and conflates reasoning errors with interface failures. This paper investigates representation robustness in LLM-based mathematical problem solving by systematically varying surface representations of the same underlying problems, including story problems, word-equations, symbolic equations, and isomorphic paraphrases. Using a curated dataset of mathematically equivalent problems, we evaluate five contemporary LLMs under a direct answer generation condition. We find substantial representational sensitivity: models frequently change correctness across equivalent formulations, with nontrivial flip rates across story, symbolic, and word-equation variants. We also observe systematic regressions under isomorphic reformulations, showing that even subtle paraphrase-level changes can degrade performance despite preserved mathematical structure. We then evaluate a code-augmented condition in which models externalize reasoning as executable Python code that is run locally for validation. This interface reveals strong latent reasoning capability in some models that perform poorly under direct prompting, but it does not uniformly improve robustness. Instead, failures shift across interaction layers, from opaque reasoning errors to protocol violations and execution failures. Even when executable reasoning succeeds, representation sensitivity often persists. Overall, our results show that reasoning scaffolds do not eliminate representational brittleness, but expose new tradeoffs among correctness, reliability, latency, and cost. We argue that representation should be treated as a first-class interface design variable in LLM evaluation and deployment, especially for AI-assisted problem-solving systems.

</details>


### [79] [Attention Degradation, Function Token Anchoring, and the Limits of Attention-Based Intervention in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20524)
*Sagar Dangal,Manoj Shakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过六个跨模型实验研究Transformer注意力退化现象是否因果性限制上下文检索，发现退化主要具有描述性而非指令性，功能词的贡献来自隐藏状态计算而非注意力分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均跨位置注意力退化在Transformer解释性中被广泛报告，但尚不清楚这是否因果性地限制了上下文检索。

Method: 在GPT-2、LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B、OPT-1.3B和distilgpt2上进行六个协调实验：表征短期注意力退化（5-100 token）、分析功能词锚定机制、测试策略性插入逗号、实施Relay-Aware Attention (RAA)机制、多因素检索探测。

Result: 发现普遍的指数衰减后平台期模式（衰减率与深度反相关）；功能词锚定具有架构依赖性（OPT距离依赖、GPT-2均匀依赖、LLaMA长距离反转）；策略性插入逗号在40-80 token范围因果性减少预测退化；RAA增加注意力质量16-24%但对大多数模型无显著效果；退化率不能预测检索准确性。

Conclusion: 平均注意力退化主要是描述性而非指令性的；功能词的贡献来自于其隐藏状态计算，而非注意力分配。这对解释性方法和基于注意力的推理优化（如KV缓存驱逐）有重要意义。

Abstract: Mean cross-positional attention degradation is widely reported in transformer interpretability, yet whether it causally limits contextual retrieval remains untested. We present six coordinated experiments across GPT-2, LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B, OPT-1.3B, and distilgpt2. We first characterise short-term (5-100 token) attention degradation, finding a universal exponential-then-plateau pattern whose rate is inversely correlated with depth, with distinct layer-wise entropy signatures per architecture. Function token anchoring proves architecture-dependent: OPT-1.3B (absolute positional encoding) shows distance-dependent preposition specificity, GPT-2 shows uniform non-specific dependence, and LLaMA (RoPE) shows reversal at long distances. Strategic comma insertion at clause boundaries causally reduces prediction degradation in the 40-80 token range, with the benefit tied to syntactic boundary alignment rather than token density. We then test the mechanism causally: Relay-Aware Attention (RAA), which biases attention logits toward function token positions, verifiably increases attention mass by 16-24% yet yields null effects on GPT-2 and LLaMA-1B, preliminary harm on LLaMA-3B, and a mixed effect on OPT-1.3B that nets to approximately zero. Multi-fact retrieval probes further show that degradation rate does not predict retrieval accuracy across models. We conclude that mean attention degradation is largely descriptive rather than prescriptive: function tokens contribute through what their hidden states compute, not through the attention they receive -- with implications for interpretability methodology and attention-score-based inference optimisations such as KV-cache eviction.

</details>


### [80] [ConfidenceBench: Evaluating Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20526)
*Matthew ffrench-Constant,Daniel Yang,Xinmeng Huang,Sanyam Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConfidenceBench是一个用于评估大型语言模型（LLM）口语化置信度校准能力的基准测试，通过Brier分数在15个前沿模型上进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署环境中，流畅但错误的回答代价高昂，仅有准确性是不够的，模型还必须知道它们可能何时出错。准确性和校准是LLM可靠性中两个不同的维度。

Method: ConfidenceBench是一个校准基准测试，使用Brier score（鼓励诚实概率报告的适当评分规则）评估口语化置信度估计。置信度通过提示词获取，无需访问模型logits，适用于封闭源和开放源系统。基准包含200个私人多项选择题，涵盖四个类别：空间推理、高精度数学、单词查找和不可知问题。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview表现最佳（Brier分数均为0.103），显著优于0.1875的校准随机基线。Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite的分数为0.367，表明严重校准不足。准确性和校准在模型家族间显著分离：最准确的模型不是校准最好的，一些模型的准确性合理但Brier分数低于校准随机基线。

Conclusion: 口语化置信度校准是LLM可靠性中一个独特且实际重要的维度，与基于准确性的标准评估相辅相成，对于需要可靠输出的关键应用场景至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where fluent but incorrect answers can be costly. In these settings, accuracy alone is insufficient: models must also know when they are likely to be wrong. We present ConfidenceBench, a calibration benchmark that evaluates verbalized confidence estimates in 15 frontier LLMs using the Brier score, a proper scoring rule that incentivises truthful probability reporting. Confidence is elicited via prompting, requiring no access to model logits and making the framework applicable to both closed-source and open-source systems. The benchmark comprises 200 private multiple-choice questions across four categories: spatial reasoning, high-precision mathematics, word lookup, and unknowable questions. Across three independent runs, Claude Opus 4.6 and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieve the lowest Brier scores, both reported as 0.103. Both substantially outperform the calibrated-random baseline of 0.1875, while Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite scores 0.367, indicating severe miscalibration. Accuracy and calibration diverge substantially across model families: the most accurate model is not the best-calibrated, and several models perform worse than the calibrated-random Brier baseline despite reasonable accuracy. These results show that verbalized confidence calibration is a distinct and practically important axis of LLM reliability, complementary to standard accuracy-based evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [DynamicMCPBench: A Trace-Grounded, Effect-Scored Benchmark for LLM Agents over Live MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20531)
*Jerzy Kamiński,Ilya Galyukshev,Artem Kuznetsov,Sergey Chuprin,Kirill Redko,Aidar Shumbalov,Anna Kalyuzhnaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DynamicMCPBench是一个可重用的评估框架，用于测试LLM代理在MCP服务器上的表现，采用基于效果的评分而非最终答案，暴露了当前代理处理长链多步骤任务的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估基准存在两个脆弱性：1）评分最终答案；2）使用固定的工具列表。当数据变为实时且具有状态时，这些方法都难以适用。需要一种更稳健的评估框架来测试代理在动态环境中的能力。

Method: 框架的核心方法包括：1）生成真实目标；2）在实时环境中执行以记录成功轨迹；3）将轨迹蒸馏为路径无关的效果检查点；4）基于代理是否能重现这些效果进行评分（而非最终答案）；5）采用pass³评分（三次独立尝试全部成功才算解决）；6）在121个服务器、750个任务（15个类别，每类50个）上测试24个模型。

Result: 实验结果显示：即使是最强的代理也只能解决约50%的任务；31%的任务没有任何模型能解决；随着所需工具链长度增加，准确率从最短链的39%下降到最长链的13%；人工验证确认自动评分具有可靠性（机会校正一致性为0.76）。

Conclusion: DynamicMCPBench将基准构建转化为 practitioners 可以在自己的服务器和模型上重新运行的可操作框架，同时暴露了当前代理在处理长链多步骤代理任务时的一致性无能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed over Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, yet the benchmarks used to evaluate them score the final answer or a fixed "ground-truth" list of tools, both of which are fragile once the underlying data is live and stateful. We present DynamicMCPBench, a reusable framework rather than a fixed dataset. A practitioner can run it on their own MCP servers to test models on their own tasks, or let it collect servers automatically to measure a model's general ability to solve agentic tasks. Given the servers and any set of models, it generates realistic goals, pursues each one live to record a successful trajectory, distills that trajectory into path-agnostic effect checkpoints, and scores an agent on whether it reproduces those effects, never on the final answer. To show what the framework reveals, we run it at scale: 24 models over 121 servers and 750 tasks spread evenly over 15 task categories (50 each), where each category targets a distinct tool-use challenge of the generated questions. Each task is scored by pass^3: it counts as solved only if all three independent attempts succeed. Even the strongest agents solve only about half of the tasks, 31% of tasks are solved by no model at all, and accuracy collapses as the required tool chain grows longer (from 39% on the shortest chains to 13% on the longest). A human validation study confirms the automatic scoring is reliable (chance-corrected agreement of 0.76). DynamicMCPBench thus turns benchmark construction into something practitioners can rerun on their own servers and models, while exposing a consistent inability of current agents to handle long, multi-step agentic tasks.

</details>


### [82] [AppWorld-UL: Benchmarking Diverse Agent-User Interactions for Tool-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20536)
*Junzhi Chen,Harsh Trivedi,Jane Pan,Michael JQ Zhang,Tejas Srinivasan,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AppWorld-UL基准测试，用于评估工具使用代理与用户的交互能力，包含516个需要多样化交互的挑战性任务，Claude Opus 4.7在基准测试上仅达到48.6%的成功率，表明正确用户交互对代理成功至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法捕捉多样化的代理-用户交互，且在小环境中运行，使用有限的、通常不改变状态的API，缺乏对真实世界复杂交互场景的评估。

Method: 基于AppWorld框架和9个流行模拟应用（如Amazon、Spotify），系统地将原始任务修改为引入歧义和约束，要求多样化代理-用户交互；用户行为由LLM模拟，通过精心设计的知识边界来响应，提供比之前工作更可靠的用户行为模拟。

Result: Claude Opus 4.7在AppWorld-UL上成功率为48.6%，在更难的组合子集上为35.7%，在更严格的场景级别指标下仅21.3%，分析表明正确用户交互对成功至关重要。

Conclusion: AppWorld-UL基准测试展示了显著难度，证明了正确用户交互对工具使用代理成功的重要性，具有推动循环中工具使用代理研究发展的潜力。

Abstract: Tool-use agents that address day-to-day digital tasks such as ordering groceries must not only operate applications, but also interact with the user, e.g., to ask clarification questions, prompt for confirmation, and inform the user when the instruction is infeasible. However, current benchmarks for evaluating agent-user interactions do not capture the diversity of such interactions. Further, they operate in small environments with few, often non-state-changing, APIs. To address this gap, we introduce AppWorld-UL, a ``user-in-the-loop'' benchmark of 516 challenging tasks requiring diverse agent-user interactions. Building upon the AppWorld framework with 9 popular simulated apps like Amazon and Spotify, we systematically modify original tasks to introduce ambiguities and constraints that necessitate various types of agent-user interaction. User behavior is simulated by an LLM prompted to respond with carefully designed knowledge boundaries, offering more reliable simulation than the unconstrained or overly rigid alternatives used in prior work. Our evaluation reveals that a state-of-the-art LLM, Claude Opus 4.7, achieves only 48.6% success on AppWorld-UL, and only 35.7% on the harder, compositional subset. On the stricter, scenario-level metric, compositional task performance drops to only 21.3%. Our analysis reveals that correct user-interaction is crucial for success. This demonstrates the benchmark's difficulty and its potential to advance research on user-in-the-loop tool-use agents.

</details>


### [83] [CMI-Mem: Toward Generalizable Long-Term Memory Management via CMI-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20553)
*Yubo Wang,Qiuyu Zhao,Zenghui Sun,Shichao Dong,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Haoyang Li,Bo Zheng,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CMI-Mem是一个基于强化学习的轻量级记忆管理模型，通过结合下游QA正确性和条件互信息（CMI）作为混合奖励，解决现有方法依赖LLM判断合成QA对导致的估值偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆管理方法主要依赖LLM判断的合成问答（QA）对，导致记忆估值高度依赖于采样的查询和下游阅读器，存在评估标准不一致的局限性。

Method: 提出CMI-Mem模型，采用强化学习框架，设计混合奖励函数：同时考虑下游QA任务正确性和条件互信息（CMI）。CMI评估新对话输入相对于当前记忆状态的信息贡献，不依赖于采样的QA查询，从而补充而非替代传统的QA基础。

Result: CMI-Mem通过RL优化记忆选择策略，能够在不依赖LLM合成QA对的情况下有效评估记忆价值，实现了比仅依赖QA判断的基线方法更优的记忆管理性能。

Conclusion: CMI-Mem证明了条件互信息作为评估指标的可行性，能够与QA判断形成有效互补，为记忆管理提供了一个更稳健、更独立的评估框架，代码和模型检查点已开源。

Abstract: Memory Manager models are pivotal in agent systems. Existing methods rely predominantly on LLM-judged synthetic question-answer (QA) pairs, making memory valuation dependent on sampled queries and the downstream reader. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{CMI-Mem}, a reinforcement learning(RL)-based lightweight memory manager model with a hybrid reward that combines downstream QA correctness and intrinsic Conditional Mutual Information (CMI). CMI evaluates the information contributed by new conversational inputs relative to the current memory state without conditioning on a sampled QA query, thereby complementing rather than replacing QA grounding. Our codes are available at: https://github.com/Wyb0627/CMIMem , and the CMI-Mem-4B model checkpoint is available at: https://www.modelscope.cn/models/wyb0627/CMIMem-4B

</details>


### [84] [WaveformQA: Benchmarking LLM Temporal Reasoning on Digital Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20638)
*Yichuan Liu,Daniel Cummings,Nick Vadlamudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WaveformQA是一个用于评估大语言模型在数字波形上时序推理能力的开源基准测试，包含360道具有程序化生成标准答案的问题，涵盖8个不同难度类别，发现LLM在简单查询上表现良好，但在复杂时序和多步骤问题上因上下文窗口限制和推理困难而性能下降，且事件时间JSON表示格式比VCD格式更能提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成和推理方面展现出强大能力，但在数字波形时序推理方面的能力仍未得到充分探索。波形推理是设计验证中的关键瓶颈，但现有基准主要评估硬件描述语言(HDL)代码生成，仅将波形作为补充上下文使用，缺乏针对时序推理的专门评估。

Method: 提出WaveformQA基准测试，包含360道具有程序化生成标准答案的问题，覆盖8个不同难度类别（包括多信号相关性和事件排序）。波形由开源设计实现程序化生成，确保可重复性和真实性。评估前沿LLM，并对比事件时间JSON表示格式与标准VCD格式对推理准确率的影响。

Result: 前沿LLM在简单查询上达到合理准确率，但在复杂时序和多步骤问题上因上下文窗口限制和推理困难导致性能下降。事件时间JSON表示格式相比VCD格式能提升LLM推理准确率。

Conclusion: 开源框架支持扩展到新的问题类别和导入新的波形源，使研究人员能够快速原型化时序推理实验。该基准揭示了当前LLM在时序推理方面的局限性，为未来改进提供了评估标准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and reasoning, yet their ability to perform temporal reasoning over digital waveform data remains largely unexplored. Although reasoning over digital waveforms is a critical bottleneck in design verification, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate hardware description language (HDL) code generation and use waveforms only as supplementary context. This paper presents WaveformQA, an open-source question-answering benchmark for evaluating LLM temporal reasoning over digital waveforms.
